MASTER A v2.0.0 · A-BATCH-3

READY_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW · 未自动 ACCEPT

BOOK VII STRUCTURED KNOWLEDGE + OBSERVATION INTERACTION CONTRACT + NO STANDALONE OBSERVATION LAB PRODUCT

100 个规范节点与 9 张 FIG 复用；17 个观察对象、1 个指标与 10 个偏差候选仍 WITHHELD。FIG 14H 主绑定 14.96，支持范围 14.85–14.95。第八册规范书名《世界将如何继续》保持不变。

1. 修正前工作17 observation objects and schemas；signal/evidence/source/measurement/comparison/uncertainty/window/bias/perspective contracts；Reality boundary, method compatibility, 9 figure links, 5 fixtures；local experimental Lab HTML/JS/data/screenshots and browser probe; never publicly activated
2. 保留structured contracts and representative fixtures；existing Ask request compatibility and authorization regression；canonical owner reuse and figure semantics；Internal R1 regression review capture: tools/review/MASTER-A-V2-A-BATCH-3-VALIDATION-CAPTURE-R1-REGRESSION-REVIEW.html; SHA-256 ccfcd73f55d207d819206973fba1e8ce372adfb0ce14433e3076ad734bf099d1
3. 撤回产品范围独立 Observation Lab 产品/UI；WITHHELD / EXPERIMENTAL / NOT_ADMITTED
4. 已创建未激活产物完整路径与修正前/当前 SHA 见下方；HTML 与 JS 已停用，截图为历史实验记录
5. 新公开路由0
6. 新 runtime owner0
7. 新 entitlement0
8. Commerce 变更0
9. providerRequests0
10. A-BATCH-4NOT_RUN

现有 Ask 以后可经单独审阅提供观察模式或“继续观察这个问题”；本批仅交付交互契约。没有新的客户入口、现实写入、导航行动或 Book VIII 权威。
